([BeppeTemp/cartographer](https://github.com/BeppeTemp/cartographer/tree/HEAD/internal/skillbundle/bundled/cartographer-feedback) · ★68)
- **cartographer-impact-workflow**: Impact-before-edit: risk tiers (CRITICAL/HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW), safe-edit sequence. Use before editing in an indexed repo. - **cartographer-implement-plan**: Use when the user asks to implement, ship or land one or more approved plans (plan files the maintainer gives) — plans the waves from the cross-plan execution order, delegates each change of a plan to a coding subagent in an isolated worktree, reviews it on its branch, and queues it for the lander in order. Sibling of `cartographer-propose-plan`, which writes the plans this skill consumes (plan → landed commits). ([BeppeTemp/cartographer](https://github.com/BeppeTemp/cartographer/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cartographer-implement-plan) · ★68)
- **cartographer-implementer-session-primer**: Prime a fresh implementer session on the Code Cartographer MCP. ([Muggler2k/code-cartographer-mcp](https://github.com/Muggler2k/code-cartographer-mcp/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cartographer-implementer-session-primer))

([BeppeTemp/cartographer](https://github.com/BeppeTemp/cartographer/tree/HEAD/internal/skillbundle/bundled/cartographer-kb-create) · ★68)
- **cartographer-kb-doctor**: Keep a Knowledge Base from rotting - a budgeted session that applies mechanical repairs and works through the server's ranked review list, always started by the operator, who decides each item or delegates the session. ([BeppeTemp/cartographer](https://github.com/BeppeTemp/cartographer/tree/HEAD/internal/skillbundle/bundled/cartographer-kb-doctor) · ★68)
- **cartographer-kb-import**: Agent-guided procedure to import an existing non-OKF wiki or knowledge base (Obsidian vault, markdown folder, wiki export) into a Cartographer KB, incrementally and without big-bang LLM rewriting. ([BeppeTemp/cartographer](https://github.com/BeppeTemp/cartographer/tree/HEAD/internal/skillbundle/bundled/cartographer-kb-import) · ★68)
- **cartographer-kb-ingest**: Ingest ONE primary source of any kind (document, meeting transcript or notes, chat thread, ticket or issue, incident timeline, email, web page, dataset) into an existing Cartographer KB - register it, merge its content into the pages that own the subject, record unknowns and contradictions, and leave a trace of which pages came from which source. ([BeppeTemp/cartographer](https://github.com/BeppeTemp/cartographer/tree/HEAD/internal/skillbundle/bundled/cartographer-kb-ingest) · ★68)
- **cartographer-kb-ingest-plan**: Use when the operator wants to ingest MANY sources (a documentation corpus, a ticket archive, a set of feeds) into a Cartographer KB as one campaign - plan it once (Maps, subject ownership, one self-contained unit per source), get the plan approved, then drive a pool of subagents over the unit queue and review a sample. For one source use cartographer-kb-ingest. ([BeppeTemp/cartographer](https://github.com/BeppeTemp/cartographer/tree/HEAD/internal/skillbundle/bundled/cartographer-kb-ingest-plan) · ★68)
- **cartographer-kb-source-author**: Use when the operator wants a NEW live source (a wiki space, a tracker project, a document library, a cluster or cloud account) followed by the Surveyor, and asks you to set it up - find the way in, write the lister script and the Surveyor config snippet, run the dry test, register the Source, extend the IngestPlan and hand over the deploy change. Started by the operator only. For ingesting one document use cartographer-kb-ingest. ([BeppeTemp/cartographer](https://github.com/BeppeTemp/cartographer/tree/HEAD/internal/skillbundle/bundled/cartographer-kb-source-author) · ★68)

- **cartographer-tuning**: Google Cartographer performs 2D/3D SLAM using a graph-based approach with two distinct subsystems: **local SLAM** (trajectory builder) builds submaps from consecutive scans, and **global SLAM** (pose graph) connects submaps via loop closures and optimizes the full graph. Configuration is done via a `.lua` file passed as a launch parameter. - **cartography-gis**: Map design, spatial analysis, and Geographic Information Systems. Covers map projections and their distortion trade-offs, coordinate systems, thematic mapping techniques (choropleth, proportional symbol, dot density, isoline), remote sensing and satellite imagery, GIS data models (vector and raster), spatial analysis operations (overlay, buffer, interpolation, network analysis), and cartographic design principles. Use when creating maps, analyzing spatial data, selecting projections, interpreting satellite imagery, or reasoning about spatial relationships in any domain.
